[GAME] Welcome to Secret Mafia! You are Player 3.
Your role: Villager
Team: Village
Description: A regular villager. Your goal is to identify and eliminate all Mafia members through voting during the day.

Players: Player 0, Player 1, Player 2, Player 3, Player 4, Player 5, Player 6, Player 7

The game progresses through Day and Night phases.
- During the Day phase, there are 3 rounds of discussion followed by voting.
- During discussions, everything you say is automatically broadcasted to all players.
- After discussions, all players must vote to eliminate one player.
- During the Night phase, you have no special actions.

The game ends when either all Mafia members are eliminated (Village wins) or
Mafia members equal or outnumber Villagers (Mafia wins).

So, I have to ask myself: **Why would a Village player suddenly claim to be on the Mafia?** That doesn’t make sense unless they are trying to **mislead us** or **throw suspicion on themselves**.

Let me also consider the **Doctor's role**. I protected Player 3 last night, and they are safe. That means I **cannot be the killer**, and I **cannot be on the Mafia**. So if I were on the Mafia, I would not be protecting someone, and I would not be trying to help the Village win.

So, to sum up:

- **Player 2's claim is suspicious.**
- **Player 6 is the Detective and confirmed Player 3 is not Mafia.**
- **I, as the Doctor, have no reason to be on the Mafia.**

Let’s keep this in mind as we move forward. I think we should be cautious of anyone who makes **bold claims** or **acts too confidently**—especially if they are trying to **shift suspicion** onto others.

**Who else do you think might be on the Mafia?**  
**Do you believe Player 2 is telling the truth?**

- After discussions, all players must vote to eliminate one player.
- During the Night phase, you have no special actions.

The game ends when either all Mafia members are eliminated (Village wins) or
